Palette of warm-ivory silk ground, charred-ink linear anchor, rouge and cinnabar for skin washes, lips, and pomegranate seeds, malachite and flower green for jade display and broker's skirt, moon-white for under-robes and jacket lining, sandalwood brown for couch frame and cabinet, lotus-root pink for couch cover, with restrained shell-white on wine cups and pearl hairpin; contour-enclosed color fields maintain narrow tonal spans, exactly three bounded skin tones of shell-white base, transparent warm flesh wash at cheeks, palms, broker's inner thigh where the merchant wife's hand rests, and contact areas, narrow deeper line-wash beneath jaws and where the merchant wife's fingers press her own breast. The uneven warm-tea field reaches every canvas edge without margin or frame; one broken asymmetrical tea-brown oxidation patch dissolves inward from the lower edge near left corner, not reaching the figures; one rubbed upper-right corner shows exposed silk weft; one shallow age crease crosses the cabinet front; three localized changes include jewel-like protected malachite in a sheltered jade recess, slight pigment flake near the fallen hairpin on couch edge, and faded gamboge in butterfly wing wash; abraded silk fibers at left edge below oxidation patch dissolve inward separately.
